## T-330 [must] lane=hull milestone=M5 required
goal: [v0.5.2 S3] A voyage's stations and each task's station are written in the plan (M5 kickoff, S3). Today crates/gear-app/src/draft.rs fn milestone_fields reads only `done:`, `bar:` and `eta:` from a `# Milestone` block (other lines are ignored) and fn parse_block_header refuses any header token but `lane=`, `milestone=` and `required`. The plan form, no other design: in the `# Milestone <id>: <title>` block, zero or more lines `station <id>: <what reaching it needs>`, in order (e.g. `station S1: reliability fixes and conventions as configuration, released as v0.5.0`); a queue header may carry `station=<id>`. A station is reached when every task of it is merged (the board task computes that). Change, no other design: (1) crates/gear-app/src/draft.rs: `pub fn milestone_stations(body: &str) -> Result<Vec<gear_store::VoyageStation>, String>` reads every trimmed line that starts with "station ": the text before the first ':' (trimmed) is the id — non-empty, no whitespace — and the rest (trimmed) is `needs`, non-empty; a repeated id, an empty id or an empty needs is an Err naming the line's text (`station <id>: <what reaching it needs>, got "<line>"`); `QueueItem` gains `pub station: Option<String>`, set by a `station=<id>` header token in fn parse_block_header (repeated → "repeated `station=` in one queue block header", empty → "`station=` needs a station id"). (2) crates/gear-app/src/plan.rs fn record_voyages: after `tx.upsert_voyage`, `tx.set_voyage_stations(project, id, &stations)` with the block's `milestone_stations(body)` (an Err is `BadFormat` "milestone <id>: <err>"). (3) plan.rs: a new `fn record_task_stations(tx, project, draft, created: &[String]) -> GearResult<()>`, called in fn apply's request closure right after `record_voyages`, with `&result.created`: it walks `draft.queue` in order, the n-th `## NEW` item taking `created[n]`, an existing item its id; an item with `station` Some(s) needs a milestone (the item's `milestone`, else the stored task's) whose `tx.voyage_stations` has the id s, else `BadFormat` "<task> names station <s>, but milestone <M> has no `station <s>:` line" (or "<task> names station <s> but has no milestone"); then `tx.set_task_station(project, task, Some(s))`; an item without `station` clears it (`None`), since a block always carries every field. (4) plan.rs fn show prints ` station=<id>` after ` milestone=<M>` in the header when `tx.task_station` is Some. (5) skills/gear/references/captain.md, "Sections and fields": the `# Milestone` item adds "optional `station <id>: <what reaching it needs>` lines, in order: the voyage's stations; a station is reached when all its tasks are merged", and the queue header becomes `## <T-<n>|NEW> [now|must|should|could|wont] lane=<lane> milestone=<M> [station=<id>] [required]`. The code change is in crates/gear-app/src/draft.rs and plan.rs and skills/gear/references/captain.md only; tests go where the crate keeps them.
accept: Draft tests: a block with two station lines gives both in order; a repeated id, `station : x` and `station S1:` are refused; a header with `station=S2` sets `station`, a repeated or empty one is refused. Plan tests (crates/gear-app/tests/it/voyage_contract.rs fixtures): an apply with stations S1, S2 and a NEW task `station=S2` stores both stations and the task's station; `gear plan show` prints `station=S2`; a task naming S9 is refused with the text above and nothing is written; re-applying the block without `station=` clears the task's station. ## T-334 [must] lane=mast milestone=M5 required
goal: [v0.5.2 S3] The board's model knows the voyage's stations: which are reached, which is now, which is next, and how far each is (backlog "Stages of a long voyage on the board": "the board shows the stations, which is reached, and what the next one needs"; S4 draws it). The store keeps the stations (`tx.voyage_stations`, `tx.task_station`, from the stations store task); nothing computes their progress. A task counts as merged only when one of its attempts has a merge time (crates/gear-engine/src/waits.rs: a verdict marks a task Done before its merge lands). Change, no other design: (1) a new module crates/gear-board/src/stations.rs (`pub mod stations;` in lib.rs) with `#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)] pub enum StationState { Reached, Now, Next, Later }`, `#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)] pub struct StationProgress { pub id: String, pub needs: String, pub state: StationState, pub tasks: usize, pub merged: usize, pub left_min: i64 }` and `pub fn progress(tx: &Tx<'_>, project: &str, milestone: &str) -> GearResult<Vec<StationProgress>>`: the voyage's scope is its tasks whose state is not Draft, Cancelled or Failed (the rule of crates/gear-app/src/station.rs); a task is merged when `tx.attempt_times_of_task(project, &task.id)?` has a `merged` time; a station's tasks are the scope tasks whose `tx.task_station` is its id; `left_min` sums `crate::voyage::estimate_of` of its tasks not merged (an unknown estimate adds 0); a station is Reached when it has at least one task and all are merged; the first station not reached is Now, the next one not reached is Next, the rest Later. (2) `pub fn course(tx, project, milestone) -> GearResult<String>`: `station <id> (<reached> of <n> reached): <needs> · <left> tasks left in <id> · <voyage left> tasks left in the voyage` for the Now station; `all <n> stations reached · <voyage left> tasks left in the voyage` when every station is reached; `<voyage left> tasks left in the voyage` when the voyage has no stations (voyage left = scope tasks not merged). (3) `pub fn goal_text(tx, project, milestone) -> GearResult<Option<String>>`: None when the voyage is missing or its `done` is empty; else `Your goal, voyage <M>: done: <done> · bar: <bar> · <course>. Judge every decision against it.` (the one text every captain output uses). (4) crates/gear-board/src/lib.rs: `ProjectBoard` gains `pub stations: Vec<stations::StationProgress>`, filled at its one constructor with `stations::progress` of the measured voyage (`workload`), empty without one. The code change is in crates/gear-board/src/stations.rs and lib.rs only; tests go where the crate keeps them.
accept: Board tests (a new crates/gear-board/tests/it/stations_contract.rs registered in main.rs, one store fixture): a voyage with S1 (two tasks, both with a merged attempt), S2 (one merged, one Running with estimate 30) and S3 (one Ready) gives S1 Reached, S2 Now with left_min 30, S3 Next; a Done task without a merge time does not count as merged; `course` reads `station S2 (1 of 3 reached): <needs> · 1 tasks left in S2 · 2 tasks left in the voyage`; all merged gives `all 3 stations reached · 0 tasks left in the voyage`; no stations gives `<n> tasks left in the voyage`; `goal_text` starts with "Your goal, voyage M5: done: " and is None for a voyage without done; `project(…).stations` holds the measured voyage's stations. Run only `make check CRATE=gear-board`; the merge gate runs `make gate`.

No drawing function of the prototype changes.

## T-340 [must] lane=mast milestone=M5 required
goal: [v0.5.3 S4] The real board can be read back as the three golden planes, and a frame depends only on the store and the `now` it was built at. Today crates/gear-board/src/frame.rs `Canvas` exposes `text()`, `ansi()` and `style_at()` but no character of a cell, and fn header draws the wall clock (fn clock_now), so a frame drawn twice a minute apart differs; and `Canvas::put_styled`/`put_line` write up to the last column, while the prototype's `put` (prototype.py fn put: `addnstr(y, x, s, wd - x - 1)`) never writes the last column. Change, no other design: (1) frame.rs `Canvas` gains `pub fn char_at(&self, x: usize, y: usize) -> char` (a space outside), `pub fn width(&self) -> usize`, `pub fn height(&self) -> usize` and `pub fn light_row(&mut self, y: usize)`, which sets every cell of row y from column 1 to column width−2 (both included) to `Style { fg: None, bold: false, reverse: true }`, characters unchanged (the prototype's fn hl). (2) `Canvas::put_styled` and `Canvas::put_line` write at most `width − x − 1` characters (nothing at x ≥ width − 1), as the prototype's put does. (3) `crate::Board` gains `pub now: Ts`, set by fn build_with_agents from its `now`; fn header draws `crate::idle::clock(board.now)` where it drew `clock_now()` today, at column width − 8 as the prototype does (`wd - 8`; mast's question M-4639), and fn clock_now goes. (4) A new module crates/gear-board/src/golden.rs (`pub mod golden;` in lib.rs): `pub struct Planes { pub text: Vec<String>, pub colour: Vec<String>, pub reverse: Vec<String> }` (one String per canvas row, one char per cell) and `pub fn planes(canvas: &crate::frame::Canvas) -> Planes`: text = the cell's character; colour = `.` for no colour, `o` Ok, `w` Warn, `b` Bad, `d` Dim, `a` Acc, `m` Mag, `u` Blu, uppercase when bold, `*` when bold with no colour; reverse = `R` when reverse, else `.` — the format of T-276's golden files. The code change is in crates/gear-board/src/frame.rs, lib.rs and golden.rs only; tests go where the crate keeps them.

A board test: two `gear_board::build` calls with the same `now` 90 s apart on the wall clock give the same `frame::canvas(...).text()`, and its row 0 holds `idle::clock(now)`. Existing tests pass (expected texts that ran into the last column follow the new clipping). Run only `make check CRATE=gear-board`; the merge gate runs `make gate`.

## T-342 [must] lane=mast milestone=M5 required
goal: [v0.5.3 S4] A view paints at columns, as the prototype does, and the selected row is lit across the width in one colour (README change 13: M4's real board lit only the cells drawn with the selection, so a row showed two or three backgrounds). Today each view returns `frame::ViewRows { heading, rows, rest }` of sequential `Line`s, so a text cannot overwrite another at a column (the prototype's views put texts at fixed columns, later ones over earlier ones), the selected row is drawn with `reversed_if(picked)` on some cells only, and fn view_decisions pushes its "why:" lines into `rows`, so they count as selectable. Change, no other design: (1) every view becomes a function that paints on a canvas: `fn(project: &ProjectBoard, screen: &Screen, canvas: &mut Canvas, top: usize) -> Vec<usize>`, returning the canvas row of each selectable row in order (the picks); `pub fn view(board, screen, canvas, top) -> Vec<usize>` dispatches on `screen.tab`. The existing views are ported as they are: the heading at row `top`, each Line put with `put_line(0, row, …)` from `top + 1`, with every `reversed_if(picked)` removed; in view_decisions only the decision lines are picks, the "why:" lines are not. (2) fn canvas paints the view on a tall canvas (the screen's width, 1000 rows), then copies its rows from `top + offset` to the screen rows `top .. height − 2` (the footer row and the row above it stay the frame's), where `offset` = 0 while the selected pick's row is above `height − 2`, else the smallest offset that shows it; after the copy it calls `light_row` on the selected pick's screen row. A new `Canvas::copy_rows(&mut self, from: &Canvas, src: usize, dst: usize, count: usize)`. (3) `top` is `VIEW_ROW` for now (the tab bar task makes it the header's height). (4) lib.rs run_with: the number of picks bounds the selection; a click on a screen row selects the pick drawn there (fn scroll_of is replaced by the offset of (2), exposed as `pub fn offset_of(picks: &[usize], selected: usize, top: usize, height: usize) -> usize`). `ViewRows` is deleted. (5) crates/gear-board/src/style.rs fn lane_color cycles `[Color::Acc, Color::Mag, Color::Warn, Color::Blu]` (the M4 prototype's lane colours in its lane order hull, deck, keel, mast: README "lanes cyan, magenta, yellow, blue") instead of today's Acc, Mag, Blu, Ok, Warn. The code change is in crates/gear-board/src/frame.rs, style.rs, graph.rs (fn styled_lines' callers only) and lib.rs only; tests go where the crate keeps them.
accept: Board tests: in Now with lane 2 selected, every cell of that screen row from column 1 to width−2 is reverse with no colour and no bold, and no other row has reverse cells outside the tab row; in Log with two decisions that both have a why, Down moves from the first decision to the second (the why line is skipped); a view with more picks than screen rows scrolls so the selected one is drawn, and a click on it keeps it selected; `lane_color(&["a","b","c","d","e"], "c")` is Warn and "e" is Acc. Existing tests pass after their expected styles follow (the selection is the lit row). Run only `make check CRATE=gear-board`; the merge gate runs `make gate`.

## T-343 [must] lane=mast milestone=M5 required
goal: [v0.5.3 S4] A test helper loads the prototype's fake data (gear/voyages/M5/board/golden/fixture.json, written by T-339) into an in-memory store with the store's own API, so the real board is drawn from the data the golden frames were drawn from. Chosen over a hand-written Rust copy of the data: the JSON is written by the prototype itself, so a change of the agreed data reaches the test without a second copy to keep in step. This task seeds the project, the voyages and every task; the decisions, frictions, quota and the reviewer are T-344. Change, no other design: a new file crates/gear-board/tests/it/golden_fixture.rs (`mod golden_fixture;` in tests/it/main.rs) with `pub fn load() -> (gear_store::Store, i64)` returning the store and `now`; it reads `concat!(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"), "/../../gear/voyages/M5/board/golden/fixture.json")` with serde_json. (1) now: the one ts in [1_793_197_920 − 43_200, 1_793_197_920 + 43_200) with ts % 60 == 0 for which `gear_board::idle::clock(ts)` equals the fixture's "now" (search minute by minute; this makes the clocks right in any time zone without touching TZ); `t(m) = now + m * 60`. (2) `testing::seed_project(tx, "gear", "s", now)`; the captain `testing::seed_active_seat(tx, "gear", "captain", SeatRole::Captain, "w1:p0", "claude", now)`; one lane seat per lane name, in the order names first appear in the voyages' LANES lists (hull, mast, keel), `seed_active_seat(tx, "gear", <name>, SeatRole::Lane, "w1:p<i+1>", "pi", now)`. (3) Voyage i (in fixture order: M5, M4.2, M4.1): departure = `now − used_m·60` when V.state is "sailing", else `now − 86_400·i − used_m·60`; `tx.upsert_voyage(&VoyageRow { project "gear", milestone, title, done: Some(V.goal), bar: Some(V.bar), eta: Some(format!("{eta_m}m")), departure: Some(departure), departure_tasks: Some(<tasks of T not "moved">), departure_estimate_min: Some(<their estimates summed>), updated: now })`; `d(m) = departure + m·60`. (4) Each task tuple `(id, lane, stage, title, est, actual, rounds, tokens_k, cost, deps, finished, marks)` of every voyage, rank = its 1-based position counting through all voyages in order: a stage "moved" seeds no task row (T-344 records it); every other one gets `insert_task` (the TaskRow of colour_contract.rs fn task with project "gear", milestone Some(V.milestone), lane Some(lane), deps, estimate_min Some(est), rank) and `insert_contract` (version 1, goal = title, every other field empty or None) and, by stage, with WHEN's `(start, merge)` minutes as s and m (attempts `AttemptRow` as colour_contract.rs fn attempt with seat "gear:<lane>", n as said; "times" are `set_attempt_time` steps): done → state Done; attempts 1..=rounds, all Rejected but the last Accepted; attempt 1 Delivered and Started at d(s); every Rejected attempt HandedIn at d(s); the last attempt Started at d(s) (when it is not attempt 1), HandedIn, Verdict and Settled at d(m − review_merge_m), Merged at d(m); a `VerdictRow` passed for the last attempt and one failed for each Rejected one (by_seat "gear:captain", candidate "c", contract_version 1, created at the HandedIn time). review → Verifying; attempt 1 Submitted, Started d(s), HandedIn `now − 12·60`. rework → Running; attempts 1..=rounds: all but the last Rejected (Started d(s), HandedIn d(s), a failed VerdictRow), the last Started at `now`. working → Running; attempt 1 Started d(s). passed → Verifying; attempt 1 Accepted, Started d(s), HandedIn and Verdict at `d(s + actual)`, a passed VerdictRow. deps and queued → Ready, no attempt. (5) Rounds, for a task with tokens_k > 0: a dev round `NewRound { key: (project, task, 1, "dev"), seat "gear:<lane>", generation 1, cli "pi", model None, start_at: d(s) }` with `set_round_active(actual·60)`, `set_round_tokens(&key, &RoundTokens { input: Some(floor(tokens_k·0.75)·1000), output: Some(0), cache_read: Some(0), cache_write: Some(0), reasoning: None, cost_usd: Some(cost·0.75), cost_source: "agent" }, now)` and `close_round(d(s) + actual·60)` unless the stage is working or rework. The review round is T-344's. The code change is in crates/gear-board/tests/it/golden_fixture.rs and main.rs only.
accept: Tests in golden_fixture.rs: `load()` gives a store whose `idle::clock(now)` is "14:32"; project gear has the 12 M5 tasks with T-262 Verifying and T-265 Ready; `attempt_times_of_task(T-257)` has Started `now − 207·60` and Merged `now − 181·60`; T-260 has two attempts, the first Rejected; T-264's dev round has active_s 1320 and input 285_000; M4.2 has no row for T-182. Run only `make check CRATE=gear-board`; the merge gate runs `make gate`.
deps: T-339
estimate: 50

## T-344 [must] lane=mast milestone=M5 required
goal: [v0.5.3 S4] The fixture loader (T-343) seeds the rest of what the prototype draws: the reviewer, the review rounds, the decisions, the friction reports, the quota readings, the goal check and the moved task. Change crates/gear-board/tests/it/golden_fixture.rs only, no other design: (1) the reviewer: `testing::seed_temp_seat(tx, "gear", "review-3", "T-262", "w1:p9", "codex", now)`. (2) For a task with tokens_k > 0 a review round `(task, a, "review")` on seat "gear:review-3", cli "codex", where a = the task's last attempt that was handed in (HandedIn set), else 1: tokens input `(tokens_k − floor(tokens_k·0.75))·1000` (output, cache_read, cache_write Some(0)), cost `cost·0.25`; T-262's round starts at `now − 12·60` and stays open; every other one starts at the attempt's HandedIn time (else d(s)) and is closed at that same time. (3) Decisions of each voyage, in order, i from 0: `insert_decision_if_new(NewDecision { project "gear", key: format!("golden-{milestone}-{i}"), voyage: milestone, at: format!("2026-10-28 {at}"), kind, what, why: Some(why) unless why is "", alt: None, changes: None, star, source: "plan", line: None, plan_version: None })`. A task of stage "moved" adds `NewDecision { kind: "move", what: format!("{id} moved to {marks}"), why: None, star: false, source: "gear", key: format!("golden-move-{id}"), at: format!("2026-10-28 {now clock}"), … }` in its voyage. (4) Frictions `(task, lane, status, body)`: `insert_friction(NewFriction { project "gear", task, attempt: 1, role: "dev", seat: "gear:<lane>", body, required: false })`; a status "read" is then closed with `close_friction(project, id, "read", now)`. (5) Quota: for each entry, `tx.set_meta(&format!("quota.reading.{provider}"), &json!({"provider": provider, "windows": [{"name": window, "used_percent": used, "resets_at": r}], "read_at": now}).to_string())` with r = for "HH:MM" the first ts > now, minute by minute, with `idle::clock(ts)` equal to it; for "Mon DD" (the "Oct 30" form) `now + 2·86_400`. (6) The goal check of each voyage: `tx.set_meta(&format!("goal-check:gear:{milestone}"), <JSON array of {"met": bool, "text": str} from DONE in order>)` (the board reads it in T-354). The stations are seeded by T-352.
accept: Tests in golden_fixture.rs: after `load()` the seat gear:review-3 holds T-262 and its open round started 12 min before now; `decisions_of("gear", "M5")` has 3 rows, two starred; M4.2 has a decision of kind "move" saying "T-182 moved to M5"; the M5 friction is open, the M4.2 one closed; the meta `quota.reading.codex` has used_percent 63; `goal-check:gear:M5` has three entries, the second met. Run only `make check CRATE=gear-board`; the merge gate runs `make gate`.
deps: T-343
estimate: 30

## T-345 [must] lane=mast milestone=M5 required
goal: [v0.5.3 S4] The gate compares the real board with the prototype cell by cell (the user, after M4's board missed its prototype's colours: "a missing colour or a wrong width fails the gate"). Change, no other design: a new file crates/gear-board/tests/it/golden_contract.rs (`mod golden_contract;` in main.rs) with: (1) `fn render(tab: Tab, detail: bool) -> gear_board::golden::Planes`: `golden_fixture::load()`, `gear_board::build(&mut store, &Options { project: Some("gear".into()), ..Default::default() }, now)`, then `board.engine = EngineState::Running` (the fixture has no engine, and the prototype draws no engine warning), `frame::canvas(&board, &Screen { tab, selected: 0, detail, size: (160, 48) }, None)`, `golden::planes`. (2) `fn golden(name: &str) -> Planes` reading `concat!(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"), "/../../gear/voyages/M5/board/golden/")` + `<name>.txt`, `.col`, `.rev` (split on '\n', a last empty piece dropped). (3) One place for what is not built yet: `struct Pending { frame: &'static str, rows: (usize, usize), cols: (usize, usize), swap: Option<(char, char)>, by: &'static str }` (inclusive ranges counted from 0) and `const PENDING: &[Pending]`. A cell inside an entry of its frame with `swap: None` (a skip entry) is not compared. A cell inside a swap entry `Some((want, got))` and inside no skip entry is compared, except that a golden colour letter `want` matches the board's `got` (case kept: `u` with `m`, `U` with `M`). (4) Eight tests, `now_folded`, `now_open`, `tasks_folded`, `tasks_open`, `cost_folded`, `cost_open`, `log_folded`, `log_open` (Tab::Now/Tasks/Cost/Log, detail false/true): every compared cell must have the same character, colour letter and reverse mark; on a difference the test fails listing at most the first 30 as `row R col C: want 'x' o . got 'y' . R`. An entry whose `by` starts with "NEW:" and all of whose cells already match (for a swap entry: no cell needed the swap) fails the test with "pending <by> on <frame> rows a-b matches now: remove the entry", so each task removes its own entries; the "prototype data" entries of (6) are never checked that way (they go when the user's data fix regenerates the frames, Open 1 of the batch). (5) PENDING starts with exactly these skip entries, each `cols: (0, 159)`: for every frame rows (0, 6) by "T-353"; folded frames rows (7, 9) by "T-354" and (10, 12) by "T-356"; open frames rows (7, 17) by "T-355" and (18, 20) by "T-356"; now-folded (13, 18) and now-open (21, 26) by "T-359", now-folded (19, 46) and now-open (27, 46) by "T-360"; tasks-folded (13, 46) and tasks-open (21, 46) by "T-362"; cost-folded (13, 46) and cost-open (21, 46) by "T-363"; log-folded (13, 46) and log-open (21, 46) by "T-364". Row 47 (the footer) is compared from the start (T-341 drew it). (6) And these entries, where the prototype's constants contradict its own formulas so no store can give both: skip entries by "prototype data: expected finish" (its projected_m 455 and slow_lane mast against its own forecast, 346 and hull): every folded frame row (8, 8) cols (43, 84) and every open frame row (13, 13) cols (51, 136); by "prototype data: merge clocks" (its `finished` clocks are 11 min before departure + WHEN's merge minute): tasks-folded rows 23, 26, 30, 33, 35 and tasks-open rows 31, 34, 38, 41, 43, each cols (144, 158); by "prototype data: T-267 stage" (queued although T-263 and T-266 are not merged): tasks-folded row (19, 19) and tasks-open row (27, 27), cols (92, 122); and swap entries `Some(('u', 'm'))` by "prototype data: lane colour" (the prototype colours mast blue by name, the board colours lanes by their order) on now-folded, now-open, tasks-folded, tasks-open, cost-folded and cost-open, rows (0, 47) cols (0, 159). The code change is in crates/gear-board/tests/it/golden_contract.rs and main.rs only. Contract v2 (mast's question M-5020: T-340 already created crates/gear-board/tests/it/golden_contract.rs and its `mod` line): T-345's tests go into that existing file beside T-340's test; main.rs is unchanged.
accept: The eight tests pass with the starting PENDING list; a test-only check (`#[test] fn a_difference_is_reported`) compares two Planes that differ in one colour letter at row 3 col 7 outside any pending entry and gets the message naming `row 3 col 7`. Run only `make check CRATE=gear-board`; the merge gate runs `make gate`.
deps: T-344, T-342
estimate: 35

## T-346 [must] lane=mast milestone=M5 required
goal: [v0.5.3 S4] Each task has one stage of the sixteen the README names, from facts the store already has (backlog "Finer task stages": another project's board showed five tasks "in review" with two reviewers). Today crates/gear-board/src/graph.rs fn stage_of maps a task to `GraphStage` (Queued, Working, Review, Merging, Done): a hand-in waiting for a reviewer and one a reviewer works on are both "in review", a failed merge reads as merging, a rework shows as queued. Change, no other design: (1) graph.rs: `GraphStage` is replaced by `pub enum Stage { Deps, Queued, Delivered, Working, Asking, Handed, Review, Verdict, SentBack, Rework, Passed, Merging, MergeFailed, Merged, Failed, Moved }` (prototype.py STAGE, SYM, STAGE_COL, OPEN) with `pub fn symbol(self) -> &'static str` (○ ○ ◌ ◉ ◉ ◎ ◎ ◎ ↺ ↺ ◍ ◍ ✗ ● ✗ → in the order above), `pub fn color(self) -> Color` (Dim Dim Dim Warn Warn Acc Acc Warn Bad Warn Acc Acc Bad Ok Bad Dim) and `pub fn is_open(self) -> bool` (true for Delivered through MergeFailed in the order above, false for Deps, Queued, Merged, Failed, Moved); `pub fn words(task: &GraphTask) -> String`: "waits for {deps joined by ", "} to merge" · "queued" · "delivered, not started" · "working" · "waits for the captain's answer" · "handed in, waits for a reviewer" · "in review" · "waits for the captain's verdict" · "sent back, rework queued" · "reworking (round {rounds})" · "passed, waits to merge" · "merging (gate running)" · "merge failed, waits for the captain" · "merged" · "failed (rounds used up)" · "moved to {moved_to or "-"}"; `GraphTask` gains `pub moved_to: Option<String>` (None here) and its `stage: Stage`. (2) fn stage_of returns `Stage`, first rule that holds, with a = the task's attempt with the highest n (an Abandoned one counts as none): Done → Merged; Failed → Failed; a Rejected and no newer attempt → SentBack; Verifying with a passed `VerdictRow` for a.n (`tx.verdicts_of_task`): the task's newest Merge op (the project's ops of kind `OpKind::Merge` with this task, from `tx.ops_in_state` over every `OpState`, newest by `created` then by the number after "O-") Failed → MergeFailed, Executing, Submitted or Unknown → Merging, else Passed; Verifying with a review verdict for a.n (`tx.get_review_verdict`) → Verdict; Verifying with a Temp seat of the project in `SeatState::Active` whose task_id is the task → Review; Verifying when the project's `quota_temps` is 0 → Verdict; Verifying → Handed; a Started, Stopped or Unknown with an Open message of kind `MsgKind::Question` for this task among `tx.messages_from_since(project, &a.seat, a.started)` → Asking; a.n > 1 and Delivered → SentBack; a.n > 1 and Started, Stopped or Unknown → Rework; a Delivered → Delivered; a Started, Stopped or Unknown → Working; a Submitted → Handed; a Accepted → Passed; no attempt and a dependency whose task is not Done → Deps; else Queued. (3) Every user of GraphStage follows: fn order (Deps and Queued first, then the open stages, then Merged newest first: prototype.py fn order_of), stage_color, info, styled_lines and frame.rs fn styled_lane_panel use `Stage::symbol`, `Stage::color` and `words`. The code change is in crates/gear-board/src/graph.rs and frame.rs only; tests go where the crate keeps them.
accept: Graph tests, one store fixture built once in the test file: a task per rule above gives its stage and its words (Deps "waits for T-1 to merge"; Rework on attempt 2 "reworking (round 2)"; a passed task with a Failed merge op "merge failed, waits for the captain"; a handed-in task with no reviewer seat "handed in, waits for a reviewer" and, with an Active temp holding it, "in review"; an open question "waits for the captain's answer"); symbol and colour of each of the 16 match the table above. Existing graph tests follow the new words. Run only `make check CRATE=gear-board`; the merge gate runs `make gate`.
deps: T-342
estimate: 50

## T-347 [must] lane=mast milestone=M5 required
goal: [v0.5.3 S4] The lane lines of the board and of the captain's home page name a task's stage in the same words as the board's views (backlog "Finer task stages": "Board, Now view, lane lines and the home snapshot use the same words"). Today crates/gear-board/src/lib.rs fn lane_line prints `<id> <state_word>` ("T-3 in progress", "T-4 in review") from the task's state, and frame.rs fn styled_lane_panel prints `task.state.as_str()`. Change, no other design: (1) `TaskBoard` gains `pub stage: graph::Stage` and `pub stage_words: String`, filled in fn project_with_agents from the same `graph::stage_of` and `graph::words` as the graph (a TaskBoard is built from the task row; build its GraphTask-free words with a helper `graph::words_of(stage, deps, rounds, moved_to)` that `words` also calls). (2) fn lane_line: the current task is the first task of the lane whose stage `is_open()`, printed `<id> <stage_words>`; no open task keeps "no current task". (3) styled_lane_panel's NOW lists every task whose stage is open with its `symbol()`, colour and `stage_words`; LEFT lists the Deps and Queued ones. crates/gear-app/src/home.rs (allowed edit) needs no code change; its tests' expected lane lines follow the new words. The code change is in crates/gear-board/src/lib.rs, graph.rs and frame.rs only; tests go where the crates keep them.

Run only `make check CRATE=gear-board` and `make check CRATE=gear-app`; the merge gate runs `make gate`.

## T-348 [must] lane=mast milestone=M5 required
goal: [v0.5.3 S4] A task's actual minutes on the board are the time its lane worked on it, not the clock since its first delivery (backlog: T-241 showed "working 89/25m" where the rework took 17 min; the minutes counted delivery, review, merge wait and a failed merge). The prototype's TIME column and the forecast's pace read the work time (prototype.py t[5]). Today crates/gear-board/src/lib.rs fn actual_min_of and graph.rs fn actual_of count from the first `started` to the merge or now. Change, no other design: both return the sum of `active_s` of the task's rounds whose role is "dev" or "rework" (`tx.rounds_of_task`), divided by 60 (integer division of the summed seconds), when the task has at least one such round; a task with none keeps today's span. `GraphTask.finished` stays the merge time. crates/gear-board/src/voyage.rs (the home page's pace) is not changed. The code change is in crates/gear-board/src/lib.rs and graph.rs only; tests go where the crate keeps them.
accept: Board tests: a task with a dev round of active_s 1500 and a rework round of 600 delivered 3 h ago shows actual 35 in its TaskBoard and GraphTask; a task without rounds keeps the span. Existing tests whose tasks have rounds follow. Run only `make check CRATE=gear-board`; the merge gate runs `make gate`.
deps: T-347
estimate: 20

## T-349 [must] lane=mast milestone=M5 required
goal: [v0.5.3 S4] The board plays each lane's queue forward to say when each lane ends and how long it will sit idle (README "Four views", ENDS and TO HANDLE). The README says the engine's run-dry forecast in crates/gear-engine/src/feed.rs "does the same" and the board reuses it; it does not: feed.rs multiplies the ready count by a lane's mean seconds per attempt and knows no dependency, and gear-engine depends on gear-board, so the board cannot call it. The prototype's fn forecast (prototype.py lines 282-316) is the definition. Change, no other design: (1) a new module crates/gear-board/src/forecast.rs (`pub mod forecast;` in lib.rs): `pub struct Job { pub id: String, pub lane: String, pub stage: graph::Stage, pub est_min: i64, pub actual_min: i64, pub deps: Vec<String> }`, `pub struct LaneEnd { pub lane: String, pub end_min: i64, pub waits: Vec<(i64, String)>, pub pace: f64 }` and `pub fn forecast(jobs: &[Job], lanes: &[String], review_merge_min: i64) -> Vec<LaneEnd>` (one per lane, in `lanes` order), exactly: pace of a lane = (Σ actual / max(1, Σ est)) of its Merged jobs, clamped to 0.5..=3.0, or 1.0 with none; a map `merged` (minutes from now when a job is on dev) starts with Merged → 0, Handed, Review and Verdict → review_merge_min, Passed and Merging → 5; four passes, each over the lanes in order: free = 0, waits = [], last = 0; for each job of the lane with stage Working, Asking, Delivered, Rework or SentBack, then each with stage Queued or Deps (each group in `jobs` order): start = free; for a Queued or Deps job, ready = the largest `merged[d]` over its deps (10^6 for a dep not in the map; 0 with no deps), and when ready > free: push (ready − free, the first dep with that largest value) to waits and start = ready; left = max(est − actual, 10) when actual > 0, else est; free = start + floor(left × pace); merged[id] = free + review_merge_min; last = merged[id]; the lane's end = the largest of last and `merged` of its Handed, Review, Verdict, Passed and Merging jobs. (2) `pub fn review_merge_min(tx: &Tx<'_>, project: &str, milestone: &str) -> GearResult<i64>`: over the milestone's Done tasks, the attempt with both HandedIn and Merged times (`attempt_times_of_task`, the highest n), the mean of (merged − handed_in) in seconds, divided by 60 (integer division); 15 when no task has both. (3) `ProjectBoard` gains `pub forecast: Vec<forecast::LaneEnd>`, filled for the measured voyage while it has open work (empty otherwise) from the tasks of `project.lanes` (each lane's `tasks` are in plan order, as `tasks_of_project` gives them by rank; `project.graph` is in display order and must not be used), est and actual 0 when unknown; `TaskBoard` gains `pub deps: Vec<String>` (the task row's deps) for it. The code change is in crates/gear-board/src/forecast.rs and lib.rs only; tests go where the crate keeps them.
accept: Unit tests in forecast.rs on the prototype's M5 jobs (the twelve of gear/voyages/M5/board/prototype.py VOYS[0] T with their est, actual and deps; stages done → Merged, review → Review, rework → Rework, working → Working, passed → Passed, deps → Deps, queued → Queued; review_merge_min 15, lanes hull, mast, keel) give hull end 134 with waits [(91, "T-266")] and pace 1.18, mast 102 with no wait, keel 61 with waits [(15, "T-262")] and pace 0.9; review_merge_min of a voyage whose two merged tasks waited 10 and 20 min is 15, of one with none 15. Run only `make check CRATE=gear-board`; the merge gate runs `make gate`.
deps: T-348
estimate: 45

## T-350 [must] lane=mast milestone=M5 required
goal: [v0.5.3 S4] The board can be built for any voyage of the project, and shows that voyage only (README change 1: "the whole board then shows that voyage only"; a landed voyage keeps its data: "no 'time unknown', no empty lanes: its lanes read 'retired at the landing'"; change 6: tasks of this voyage only). Today crates/gear-board/src/lib.rs fn project_with_agents measures one voyage (fn workload_voyage) and shows its tasks plus every open task of other voyages, the lanes are the active lane seats (a retired lane's tasks are skipped), and `voyage::reports` drops a finished voyage. Change, no other design: (1) `Options` gains `pub milestone: Option<String>` (None = today's choice); fn build_with_agents passes it to a new `pub fn project_for(tx, project, now, agents: Option<&[AgentInfo]>, milestone: Option<&str>) -> GearResult<ProjectBoard>`; fn project_with_agents calls it with None (its signature and its callers, gear-app home.rs, are unchanged). (2) `pub struct VoyageEntry { pub milestone: String, pub title: String, pub sailing: bool, pub departure: Option<Ts>, pub ended: Option<Ts> }`; `ProjectBoard` gains `pub voyage_list: Vec<VoyageEntry>` (every voyage of `tx.voyages_of_project` with a departure, newest departure first) and `pub shown: Option<VoyageEntry>` (the measured one); sailing = some task of its milestone is not Done, Failed or Cancelled; ended = for a landed voyage the latest Merged time of its tasks' attempts, else None. (3) With Some(m): the measured report is m's from `voyage::reports_including_finished`; the tasks are every task whose milestone is m (no open task of another voyage), Cancelled ones left out of the lanes' tasks and the totals (a cut task is not work; fn graph::tasks leaves them out already); decisions and frictions are m's. With None: today's measured voyage and today's task rule (the home page, which calls fn project_with_agents, keeps it). fn build_with_agents (the board) always passes Some: `opts.milestone`, else the milestone fn workload_voyage measures (None only for a project without a voyage), so the board shows the tasks of its voyage only (README change 6). (4) Lanes of a landed voyage: the lanes of its tasks, retired ones included, in seat order (`seats_of_project`), a lane with no seat after them; each with `idle_text = Some("retired at the landing".into())`. A sailing voyage keeps today's lanes. The graph is filtered to the shown tasks as today. The code change is in crates/gear-board/src/lib.rs and voyage.rs only; tests go where the crate keeps them.
accept: Board tests on one fixture built once (a landed voyage M1 with a retired lane `old` that merged T-1, and a sailing M2): built with milestone None the board shows M2 only (no T-1) and voyage_list is [M2 sailing, M1 landed]; with Some("M1") it shows T-1, lane `old` with "retired at the landing", ended = T-1's merge time; decisions of M1 only. Existing board tests that expect an open task of another voyage on the board follow README change 6; gear-app home tests are unchanged. Run only `make check CRATE=gear-board`; the merge gate runs `make gate`.
deps: T-349

## T-332 [must] lane=mast milestone=M5 required
goal: [v0.5.2 S3] A goal change the user asked for and confirmed is applied through a `goal-change` line and recorded as a starred decision with before and after. The previous task refuses every change of a departed voyage's title, done, bar or stations (crates/gear-app/src/goal.rs fn check); there is no way through yet. The captain confirms such a change with the user in a confirmation dialog at the moment the user asks for it; GEAR does not store the user's words. The form, no other design: the edited `# Milestone` block carries the new words, and in the same apply `# Decisions` carries `- <YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM> goal-change: <M> <what changes> | why: confirmed by the user`; the line only authorizes, the words come from the block. Change, no other design: (1) crates/gear-app/src/decision.rs: "goal-change" joins `KINDS` (`[&str; 16]`). (2) crates/gear-store/src/lib.rs: `pub fn decision_key_exists(&self, project: &str, key: &str) -> GearResult<bool>` on `Tx` (a row of `decisions` with that project and key). (3) goal.rs fn check: a milestone with changes is authorized when `draft.decisions` holds a line whose `crate::decision::parse` kind is "goal-change", whose `what` starts with the milestone id as its first word, whose `why` is exactly "confirmed by the user", and whose key (`crate::decision::key(line)`) is not in the log yet (`tx.decision_key_exists`) — a line left from an earlier apply authorizes nothing; any other `why` is `BadFormat` "a goal-change line ends with `| why: confirmed by the user` (confirm it with the user first)"; check returns the GoalChanges of the authorized milestones and refuses (GOAL_LOCKED) only for the others; the refusal text gains the last line "When the user asks for this change, confirm it with them, then add to # Decisions: - <YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM> goal-change: <M> <what changes> | why: confirmed by the user". (4) `pub fn record(tx: &Tx<'_>, project: &str, version: i64, changes: &[GoalChange], now: Ts) -> GearResult<()>` writes, per change, `tx.insert_decision_if_new(&gear_store::NewDecision { project: project.to_string(), key: crate::decision::key_of(&format!("goal-change|{version}|{milestone}|{field}|{before}|{after}")), voyage: milestone.clone(), at: crate::decision::apply_time(now), kind: "goal-change".to_string(), what: format!("{milestone} {field}: «{before}» → «{after}»"), why: Some("confirmed by the user".to_string()), alt: None, changes: None, star: true, source: "gear".to_string(), line: None, plan_version: Some(version) })` — starred. (5) plan.rs fn apply_queue keeps the check before the decision lines are recorded and keeps the returned list (`let goal_changes = crate::goal::check(tx, project, draft)?;`) and calls `crate::goal::record(tx, project, applied_version, &goal_changes, now)?` after them; record_voyages then writes the new words as today. (6) skills/gear/references/captain.md, "Sections and fields": the `# Milestone` item adds "After departure its title, `done:`, `bar:` and station lines are locked (GOAL_LOCKED). Change them only when the user asks: confirm with them in a dialog, then in one apply edit the block and add `- <date> goal-change: <M> <what changes> | why: confirmed by the user`; GEAR records it as a ★ decision with before and after"; the `# Decisions` kinds list gains `goal-change`. The code change is in crates/gear-app/src/decision.rs, goal.rs, plan.rs, crates/gear-store/src/lib.rs and skills/gear/references/captain.md only; tests go where the crates keep them.
accept: gear-app tests (voyage_contract.rs fixtures): a departed voyage's `done:` edited with a `goal-change: M1 … | why: confirmed by the user` line applies, the stored done is the new one, and the log holds a starred decision of kind "goal-change", source "gear", what `M1 done: «…» → «…»`; re-applying the same file changes nothing and adds no decision; the next apply with the old line still there and another edit is refused (GOAL_LOCKED); a goal-change line naming M2 does not unlock M1; a line with another `why` is refused with the BadFormat text; a station change is authorized and recorded the same way; `editing_a_grandfathered_milestone_keeps_its_historical_departure` gets a variant whose third apply carries a goal-change line and applies. A store test for `decision_key_exists`. Run only `make check CRATE=gear-store` and `make check CRATE=gear-app`; the merge gate runs `make gate`.

(4) crates/gear-cli/src/main.rs, the plan apply that calls rewrite_applied, passes `&result.milestone_blocks` (an allowed edit). Existing gear-app tests whose drafts name a milestone with no block and no stored words change: crates/gear-app/tests/it/voyage_contract.rs `the_departure_is_the_first_task_creation_even_without_the_block` becomes the refusal test (a first apply without the block is refused; with the block the departure is the first task's creation), the first apply of `editing_a_grandfathered_milestone_keeps_its_historical_departure` carries the block, and the fixture drafts of crates/gear-app/tests/it/time_contract.rs, time_report_contract.rs and verdict_item_contract.rs gain a `# Milestone` block (no gear-engine, gear-board or gear-cli test applies such a draft; name every test you change in your report). The code change is in crates/gear-app/src/goal.rs, plan.rs, draft.rs and crates/gear-cli/src/main.rs only; tests go where the crates keep them.

## T-354 [must] lane=mast milestone=M5 required
goal: [v0.5.3 S4] Under the goal box the folded header gives, in three rows, the goal check, the current milestone, the time against the plan and the tasks, with bold labels, and `d` unfolds it (README changes 3, 4, 6, 14; prototype.py fn header lines 190-233). Change, no other design (W = canvas width; the puts in exactly this order, a later one over an earlier one, as the prototype paints): (1) model, crates/gear-board/src/lib.rs: `ProjectBoard.goal_check: Vec<(bool, String)>` = the meta `goal-check:<project>:<milestone>` of the shown voyage (a JSON array of `{"met": bool, "text": str}`), else the clauses of its done sentence (frame.rs fn done_clauses moves here) each unmet. (2) the numbers: used = the shown voyage's elapsed minutes while it sails, (ended − departure)/60 once landed; eta = its eta in minutes (frame.rs fn duration_minutes); expected = used + the largest `end_min` of `project.forecast` (used when empty), slow lane = the first lane with that end ("-" when empty); n = max(20, min(40, (W − 60)/2)); span = max(eta, expected, used); the bar = n cells, k = round-half-to-even(used/span · n) (`f64::round_ties_even`) cells "█" then "░", the cell min(n − 1, eta·n/span) set to "│" and, while sailing, the cell min(n − 1, expected·n/span) set to "◆" (integer divisions); sailing: diff = eta − expected, short = "expected <hm(expected)> of <hm(eta)>, <hm(diff)> ahead" or ", <hm(−diff)> behind"; landed: diff = eta − used, short = "took <hm(used)> of <hm(eta)>, <hm(diff)> under" or "over"; `hm(m)` = "<m/60>h<m%60 two digits>m" always with hours ("0h25m"; frame.rs fn hours stays for the other texts); tasks = the shown tasks but Cancelled ones, merged = stage Merged, waiting = Deps or Queued, going = the rest; a = merged·n/max(1, tasks), b = going·n/max(1, tasks). (3) rows from the row after the box, mx = max(90, W/2 + 10): row 1: "Goal check" Bold at 1; "<met> of <all> met" at 15, Ok when all are met else Bad; when one is unmet "next: <its text>" Bad at 30; "Milestone" Bold at mx; at mx + 11 Bold "▶ <id> <what> (<reached> of <N> reached)" for the first station that is Now (else the last station: "✔ all <N> reached"), "none set" Dim with no station. Row 2: "Time" Bold at 1; the bar at 15 and short at 16 + n, both Ok when diff ≥ 0 else Bad; "Tasks" Bold at mx; at mx + 11 "█"·a Ok, "▓"·b Warn, "░"·(n − a − b) Dim; at mx + 12 + n "<merged>/<tasks> merged · <going> going · <waiting> waiting" plain. Row 3: "d: unfold the goal check, milestones, time and tasks" Dim at 1. The tab row follows after one blank row, as before (the tab bar task draws its frame). The code change is in these three crates' files only; tests go where the crates keep them.

goal: [v0.5.0 S1] The board does no work while nothing changes. Today crates/gear-board/src/lib.rs fn run_with rebuilds the whole board (`build_with_agents`, every view's queries) and asks Herdr for its agents every `REFRESH` (2 s) and after every key. Change crates/gear-board/src/lib.rs fn run_with only, no other design: keep the last built board and the store's `PRAGMA data_version` (read it with one `store.read` query; add a small `pub fn data_version(&self) -> GearResult<i64>` on `gear_store::Tx` in crates/gear-store/src/lib.rs, an allowed edit) and Herdr's agent list; rebuild only when the data version changed, the agent list changed, or 60 s passed (the clock and time-based texts), and otherwise redraw keys, selection, scrolling and panels from the kept board; ask Herdr for its agents at most every 10 s. Nothing the board shows changes. The code change is in these two files only; tests go where the crates keep them.

A gear-store test for `data_version` changing after a write from another connection. Run only `make check CRATE=gear-store` and `make check CRATE=gear-board`; the merge gate runs `make gate`.

## T-385 [must] lane=mast milestone=M5 required
goal: [v0.5.0 S1] The store reuses its prepared statements, so a tick or a board frame does not re-parse every query (sampling of the engine and the board, 2026-10-29: after waiting, the largest cost is SQLite parsing — sqlite3RunParser and sqlite3GetToken — because crates/gear-store/src/lib.rs calls `self.tx.prepare(…)` on every query). Change crates/gear-store/src/lib.rs only, no other design: every `prepare(` on the connection or transaction becomes `prepare_cached(` (rusqlite's statement cache), and the connection's cache capacity is set to 256 where the connection is opened (`set_prepared_statement_cache_capacity`). No query text or result changes. The code change is in crates/gear-store/src/lib.rs only.
accept: `grep -c "\.prepare(" crates/gear-store/src/lib.rs` prints 0 outside test code; the existing store tests pass unchanged. Run only `make check CRATE=gear-store`; the merge gate runs `make gate`.
mechanical: a call-for-call rename of prepare to prepare_cached plus one capacity line; the existing store suite proves it

## T-407 [must] lane=hull milestone=M5 required
goal: [v0.5.0 S1] The engine logs how long its multi-step operations take, so a slow one is found from the log (2026-10-29: a `/new` session switch took a minute and nothing in the log said which step was slow; the cause was a 3-13 s store write in the overrun step that every step waited behind). Change, no other design: one engine-log line per finished operation and per step, `probe <op> <seat or task> <step> <ms> total=<ms>`, written through the same tick log the engine already writes (as T-398 does for releases): (1) a session switch or resume (crates/gear-engine/src/session_runner.rs: each step from its `step_at` to the next, and the whole from the first step to `done`); (2) a delivery (crates/gear-engine/src/ops.rs: typed, Enter sent, confirmed or failed); (3) a lane restart onto another model or CLI (crates/gear-engine/src/restart.rs: each Phase); (4) a merge (crates/gear-engine/src/merge.rs: fetch, merge, gate, push, close branches); (5) every store write over 500 ms keeps its existing line. The step times come from the durable state these modules already keep (their `step_at` and op times), so an engine restart in the middle still logs the right durations. The code change is in those four files only (allowed edits for mast); tests go where the crate keeps them. Start this branch from hull/t386, the previous link of this chain, handed in but not merged yet: git fetch origin && git switch -c <this branch> origin/hull/t386 (if it exists already: git switch <this branch>), then merge origin/dev into it as usual; its diff against origin/dev includes that link's commits until it merges, and the reviewer judges only this task's own commits.
accept: (Moved to hull for balance at station M-5296: the files this task names are allowed edits for hull.) Engine tests with FakeHerdr: a session switch to done logs one probe line per step and one total; a delivery confirmed after 40 s logs `confirmed 40000`; a merge logs its five steps. Run only `make check CRATE=gear-engine`; the merge gate runs `make gate`.
estimate: 40

## T-386 [must] lane=hull milestone=M5 required
goal: [v0.5.0 S1] The engine says what each tick costs, and each tick reads its branches with one git call per repository instead of one per task. Today crates/gear-engine/src/review.rs fn start_direction_checks runs `git rev-parse --verify refs/remotes/<remote>/<branch>` for every started attempt on every tick, and crates/gear-engine/src/quiet.rs runs `git log -1 … refs/remotes/<remote>/<branch>` per lane per tick, each a process. Change, after T-380 (review.rs) and T-292 (quiet.rs), no other design: (1) crates/gear-engine/src/lib.rs fn tick measures each of its steps with `std::time::Instant` and, for a step over 200 ms, adds `<step>: <ms> ms` to a new `TickReport.slow: Vec<String>` (printed with the report as today); (2) a new `pub(crate) fn branch_tips(cfg: &EngineCfg, repo: &str, remote: &str) -> HashMap<String, (String, Ts)>` in crates/gear-engine/src/review.rs running one `git for-each-ref --format=%(refname:short) %(objectname) %(committerdate:unix) refs/remotes/<remote>/` per repository and tick (through `review_git` as today), and fn start_direction_checks and quiet.rs's push-time read take their sha and time from it instead of their per-branch calls. Behaviour is unchanged. The code change is in lib.rs, review.rs and quiet.rs only; tests go where the crate keeps them.
accept: (Moved to hull for balance at station M-5296: the files this task names are allowed edits for hull.) Engine tests: `branch_tips` on a fixture repo returns each remote branch's sha and commit time; a tick over two started attempts makes one for-each-ref call per repository and no rev-parse (the fake git records its calls); a tick step forced over 200 ms appears in `slow`; the existing direction and quiet tests pass. Run only `make check CRATE=gear-engine TESTS='<a nextest filter: the tests of the module you changed and your new test>'`; the merge gate runs `make gate`.
deps: T-380, T-292
estimate: 40

## T-387 [must] lane=mast milestone=M5 required
goal: [v0.5.0 S1] A voyage whose `eta:` is not a duration does not depart. Change, no other design: (1) crates/gear-app/src/plan.rs fn record_voyages refuses, with nothing written, a milestone block whose `eta:` is not wholly a duration (fn eta_seconds returns None: one or more `<number><unit>` groups and nothing else) with `bad(…, "milestone <M>: eta must be a duration GEAR measures the voyage against, e.g. `eta: 8h` or `eta: 1d 4h`; reasons go in # Decisions; got \"<the first 40 characters>\"")`; this holds for every block, departed or not, so a voyage with such an eta cannot depart and a plan that carries one cannot be applied (fn eta_seconds is made `pub(crate)` or moved where both use it); (2) skills/gear/references/captain.md, where `eta:` is described: "a duration (`8h`, `1d 4h`) and nothing else: apply refuses any other text, since the board, the stations and the forecast measure against it" (an allowed edit). fn eta_seconds itself stays strict. The code change is in plan.rs, station.rs and captain.md only; tests go where the crates keep them.
accept: Plan tests: a block with `eta: 8h` or `eta: 1d 4h` applies; `eta: 8h, hard` and `eta: do it properly` are refused with the text and nothing is written (no task, no voyage, no departure). Existing fixtures that use a style word as eta change to a duration. Run only `make check CRATE=gear-app`; the merge gate runs `make gate`.
estimate: 20

## T-388 [must] lane=mast milestone=M5 required
goal: [v0.5.0 S1] `gear admin init --check` shows what a re-init would change (another project, 2026-10-29: a whole-config re-init once dropped the user's chosen review model without a word, and a captain cannot see before the re-init what the store will become). Change crates/gear-cli/src/main.rs fn cmd_admin_init only, no other design: with `--check`, when the store the call would use exists (the same read-only rule as `other_projects`) and holds a project with the file's id, print after the "config ok" line `changes against the registered config v<n>:` followed by a line-by-line diff of the registered `config_toml` and the file's text (`- ` for a line only in the registered one, `+ ` for a line only in the file, in file order; a simple LCS over lines, no new dependency), or `no change against the registered config v<n>` when equal; `--json` gains `"changes": ["- …", "+ …"]`. Nothing is written. The code change is in main.rs only; tests go where the crate keeps them.
accept: CLI tests: a registered project whose file changes its review model line prints the "- " old line and the "+ " new line; an identical file prints "no change"; a project not registered prints neither; nothing is written. Run only `make check CRATE=gear-cli`; the merge gate runs `make gate`.
estimate: 25

## T-389 [must] lane=mast milestone=M5 required
goal: [v0.5.0 S1] The skill tells every captain to cap the lanes' builds through the project's Makefile. Text changes only, exactly these: (1) skills/gear/references/captain.md, §1 Kickoff step 4 ("Write the config"), after its first sentence: "Before departure the repository has a Makefile (or the stack's equivalent) with one target per cost, and the gate is one of them: `check` — what a lane or reviewer runs: one package or crate, given as an argument it refuses to run without, with its build jobs and test threads capped (2 and 2 on a shared machine); `gate` — the merge gate the engine runs once per merge: the whole build and suite, still capped; `full` — uncapped, run by you alone for a release. Lanes and reviewers never build or test the whole repository themselves." (2) skills/gear/references/decompose.md, §2 Writing a task, the `accept` bullet: add "It names the exact capped check the lane runs (`Run only \`make check <the package this task changes>\`; the merge gate runs \`make gate\``), never a whole-repo build or test." (3) skills/gear/references/rules/lane.md, the line "Before handing in: Run the packet's acceptance commands…": it already says never `make full`; add "and never a whole-repository build or test of your own." The code change is these three files only.
accept: The three texts read as above (`gear --skill captain`, `gear --skill decompose`, the lane rules); the skill topic tests pass. Run only `make check CRATE=gear-app`; the merge gate runs `make gate`.
estimate: 10
